	private IConnectionPointContainer m_ConnectionPointContainer;

	private ArrayList m_aEventSinkHelpers;

	private IConnectionPoint m_ConnectionPoint;

	private void Init()
	{
		IConnectionPoint ppCP = null;
		Guid riid = new Guid(new byte[16]
		{
			99, 155, 205, 14, 170, 35, 208, 17, 179, 81,
			0, 160, 201, 5, 93, 142
		});
		m_ConnectionPointContainer.FindConnectionPoint(ref riid, out ppCP);
		m_ConnectionPoint = ppCP;
		m_aEventSinkHelpers = new ArrayList();
	}

	public void add_Click(DMSHFlexGridEvents_ClickEventHandler P_0)
	{
		bool lockTaken = default(bool);

		{
			Monitor.Enter(this, ref lockTaken);

			{
				Init();
			}

			int pdwCookie = 0;
			m_ConnectionPoint.Advise(dMSHFlexGridEvents_SinkHelper, out pdwCookie);
			dMSHFlexGridEvents_SinkHelper.m_dwCookie = pdwCookie;
			dMSHFlexGridEvents_SinkHelper.m_ClickDelegate = P_0;
			m_aEventSinkHelpers.Add(dMSHFlexGridEvents_SinkHelper);
		}
		finally
		{
			if (lockTaken)
			{
				Monitor.Exit(this);
			}
		}
	}

		bool lockTaken = default(bool);

			if (0 >= count)
